# Mutation checks: each line breaks one critical check, and the listed
# command must then fail. scripts/mutate.sh applies them one at a time in a
# scratch copy of the tree.
#
# Format, tab-separated:  file  search  replacement  command
# The search text is literal and must occur exactly once in the file. A
# mutation must still compile: a build failure is reported, not counted.
